+//! Deterministic role populations validated by the ordinary seed boundary.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#132-generated-initial-worlds-and-password-only-test — generated initialization is explicit.
+use super::*;
+/// No network, credentials, development doors, durable store or scratch files.
+pub fn fixture(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
+    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fixture world not reserved; remaining initialization uncovered".into());}
+    let mut people=Vec::new();
+    for (table,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
+        let mut role_columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
+        role_columns.extend(["role","kind"].into_iter().filter(|c|t.columns.contains_key(*c)).map(str::to_owned));
+        for column in role_columns{
+            {
+                for role in super::super::privilege::role_values(bound,table,&column) {for n in 0..2 {people.push((table.clone(),column.clone(),role.clone(),format!("account:security-{table}-{role}-{n}")));}}
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for seat in histories::viewers(bound).into_iter().filter(|p|p.starts_with("service:")) {for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-{}-{n}",seat.replace(':',"-"))));}}
+    if people.is_empty(){for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-owner-{n}")));}}
+    if people.len()>64{people.truncate(64);out.unsupported.insert("generated roster capped at 64 principals; additional roles remain uncovered".into());}
+    let mut grant_tables=BTreeSet::new();
+    fn grants(rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
+        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|v|matches!(v,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{out.insert(table.clone());},
+        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{grants(r,out)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>grants(r,out),_=>{}
+    }}
+    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){grants(&t.rules.read,&mut grant_tables);}
+    let counts=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()).map(|(table,t)|{
+        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).count();(table.clone(),if own>0{own}else if grant_tables.contains(table)||t.rules.read.any_term(&|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if t.columns.get(c).is_some_and(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)))){people.len().max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3))}else{t.columns.values().filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(2).max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3)).clamp(2,8)})
+    }).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
+    let mut rows=BTreeMap::new();
+    for (table,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
+        if !counts.contains_key(table){continue;}
+        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+        if t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count()>3 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: full Cartesian combinations above three principal columns remain uncovered"));}
+        let mut population=Vec::new();
+        for n in 0..counts[table]{
+            let person=if own.is_empty(){&people[n%people.len()]}else{own[n]};
+            let mut fields=BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),Value::Id(format!("security-{table}-{n}")))]);
+            let mut principal=0;
+            for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
+                if column=="id"||t.engine_field(column)||t.recorded.contains_key(column)||t.maintained.contains_key(column){continue;}
+                let mut value=if kind.is_optional()&&n%2==0{Value::Null}else{match sample(kind.required(),4+n){Some(value)=>value,None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}.{column}: unsupported typed sample"));continue;}}};
+                if value==Value::Null&&kind.is_optional(){fields.insert(column.clone(),value);continue;}
+                match kind.required(){
+                    C::Principal=>{
+                        let linked=!own.is_empty()||grant_tables.contains(table)||t.constraints.iter().any(|constraint|matches!(constraint,Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{key,..} if key.iter().any(|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column))));
+                        value=Value::Id(if linked{if principal==0{person.3.clone()}else{people[(n+principal)%people.len()].3.clone()}}else{if (n>>principal.min(5))&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()});principal+=1;
+                    },
+                    C::Ref{table:target}=>{value=Value::Id(format!("security-{target}-{}",n%counts.get(target).copied().unwrap_or(1)));},
+                    C::Enum{..}|C::Text{..} if person.0==*table&&person.1==*column=>value=Value::String(person.2.clone()),
+                    C::Text{max}=>{let minimum=t.constraints.iter().filter_map(|c|if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=c{(field==column).then_some(*min as usize)}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(1);let suffix=format!("sample{n}");value=Value::String(format!("{suffix}{}","x".repeat(minimum.saturating_sub(suffix.len()))).chars().take(*max as usize).collect());},
+                    C::Time=>value=Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_deref()==Some(column){86_400_000}else{-1000}),
+                    _=>{},
+                }
+                fields.insert(column.clone(),value);
+            }
+            population.push(Value::Row(fields.into()));
+        }
+        rows.insert(table.clone(),population);
+    }
+    // Batch validation preserves final-exists, parent ordering and maintains.
+    if let Ok(engine)=engine_with(bound,&rows,Vec::new()){return Ok(engine);}
+    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fallback world not reserved; initialization uncovered".into());}
+    let mut engine=engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new())?;
+    let mut pending=rows.into_iter().flat_map(|(table,rows)|rows.into_iter().map(move|row|(table.clone(),row))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+    let mut reasons=BTreeMap::new();
+    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
+        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
+        for (table,mut row) in pending {
+            if !out.reserve(1){validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
+            let attempt=(||{
+                for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[&table].columns {if let Ok(value)=row.field(column){let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,value)?;if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut row{fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}}}
+                engine.seed_at([(table.clone(),row.clone())],NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;Ok::<_,String>(())
+            })();
+            if let Err(why)=attempt{reasons.insert(table.clone(),why);retry.push((table,row));}
+        }
+        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
+    }
+    for (table,why) in reasons{if pending.iter().any(|(name,_)|name==&table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: some related rows declined by ordinary validation: {why}"));}}
+    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;
+    Ok(engine)
+}

### 12.8 Runtime intent and independent worlds

Reference-hop intent is judged by the engine's own authorized RuleProbe and
three-valued verdict on an isolated actual snapshot. Hidden/missing references
remain Unknown, never false; snapshot-only evaluators decline those terms.
Temporal reference policies require a per-tick authorized replay and remain
explicitly unsupported until that path runs. Referenced immutable-column worlds
use fresh stores with copied verified asset ledgers and all authored base rows,
then ordinary seed validation/recomputed maintenance. They do not require a
legal delete transition. Every non-protected base field, capture, row identity
and row population must match before comparing observations.

Implicit stability includes source and member writes to row-keyed views,
maintained owner columns, through-parent effects and transitive cascade/set-null.
Only an actual direct delete can refute delete authority; commit-end expiry is
not relabeled a deletion by the caller. Defaulted upsert roles are analyzed on
the insert arm only; omitted fields are preserved on update. Revocability advice
requires an expanded write to a mutable role field or an admitted deletion,
not an unrelated display-name update.

For a write history, a stable necessary policy component may establish a
violation despite another changing conjunct. Replace unstable atoms (or an
entire unstable negation) by Allow, retaining And/Or composition; separately
verify `original => weakened` with the existing exhaustive kernel. Denial of
that weakened condition establishes denial of the original, never its proof.
Stability includes transitive read-authority dependencies of referenced rows.
Do not use this reduction when implicit writes can change the target image or
executed arm. This catches a demoted recorder whose role stays false even when
another statement updates an unrelated field on its parent. No new axiom is
introduced. Id-column presence/count intent shares the row identity observer
path; arbitrary untyped matching ids do not establish a payload witness.

Independent intent binds authorized reference operands without installing a
runtime read gate; live-hop restrictions on executable read rules do not remove
those operands from intent. Probe-key/equality reference hops participate in
stability and transitive authority dependency collection. Every committed
history state rechecks row assertions, including unchanged rows whose related
policy rows changed; pre-existing unchanged seed violations stay supplemental.
Role advice expands helpers with lexical argument substitution and dominating
requirements, retaining the actual write source site. Constant defaults and
literal assignments use the same authority comparison. Optional-column pairs
vary both values and null presence to observe value-existence aggregates.

Mutation column observers include transitive read-authority dependencies and
implicit targets when establishing policy stability. Role probes normalize
unique-key components and extra equalities identically. Argument-domain
narrowing follows the resolved argument expression, never a coincidentally
same-named argument beside a literal assignment. An unconditional helper call
exports its guaranteed requirement prefix to subsequent caller writes;
conditional/loop-local requirements do not become caller assumptions.

Column observers first judge authorized intent on the validated primary world
once per actor. They share a 16384-call campaign budget (including repeatability
and declined worlds), with explicit remaining-world coverage when exhausted.
The budget cannot discharge an obligation or turn an unproved path into proof.

Only current-role intent establishes that retaining a grant is wrong; naming a
principal column after a role does not. Lifetime advice therefore requires an
independent current-role invariant as well as an authored revocation writer.
The project-wide missing-intent note identifies tables with no invariants; it
does not diagnose a missing current-role clause when other intent is present.
Without independent current-role intent, lifetime advice stays silent: authors
must declare that contract before the checker can distinguish intentional
retained access from a stale grant. Global role
advice requires a principal/role unique roster; per-resource context keys need
a separate delegation reduction. Delegation guards constrain the actual role
column of that roster, never an ordinary project/status lookup. Assignment
intent is selected by effect, with upsert arms judged separately. Initial row
assertion failures remain visible as TrustedSeedOnly, even without mutations.
The operation matrix uses a shared remaining-call counter and fair per-operation
quotas of 1–512; above 8192 eligible operations, unvisited names are explicit.

For fixed ordinary enrollment, row-scoped permissions alone do not warrant
escalation advice. Without contrary independent intent, a fixed assignment
requires new unconditional read/delete or roster authority to emit the note;
caller-chosen roles retain the broader effect comparison. This advisory is not
an all-operation reachability theorem; scoped-resource delegation and fixed
write-only privilege distinctions require independent intent/reductions.
The report keeps the shortest reachable witness. Campaigns can also retain
separate one- and two-step examples without replacing a shorter trace or
attributing a pre-existing disclosure to an unrelated mutation. Named-operation
intent covers all its target-table effects; table/column insert/update/delete
intent covers only that executed arm. Column prefilters and paired intent use
validated committed images; unsupported synthetic operation judgments are named.

History read observers first select compiler-typed row-return candidates or the
transitive maintained/through/raw-probe table closure for scalar observations.
They reserve at most 32768 authored read calls per campaign, including repeats;
remaining disclosures are explicit uncovered work. Mutation sequence replay
continues within its own bounds. Read-call exhaustion never grants a proof.

Tuple-key role tests use the same column-aware normalization in lifetime advice.
A scoped enum context is not a role qualifier; qualifying unique keys contain
one principal and actual role qualifiers only. Named-operation delegation intent
is honored for that caller operation. Initial fixture column observations run
before history prefixes and synthetic exploration; continuation shares the same
work counters so a later phase cannot evict a zero-step witness by consuming its
budget first. §12.8's independent-current-role requirement supersedes the older
name-based alternative in §12.6.

Fixed-role comparison retains any additional probe context as an opaque
predicate; a matching role alone cannot make a region-qualified read globally
unconditional. Lifetime advice excludes a retained branch that already implies
the declared policy, including explicit owner alternatives. Arbitrarily named
role qualifiers require unambiguous literal-probe evidence before a compound
principal/qualifier key can be treated as a global roster.

Synthetic enumeration deduplicates identical candidate seed rows and typed
operation arguments after dependency pruning and literal pinning. Unrelated
schema enum digits cannot cause repeated replay of the same input. This is
finite input exploration, with no exhaustive entropy/generated-id claim.

A different or negated current-role check does not discharge retained-grant
intent: only implication of the applicable independent policy suppresses that
advice. Live roster probes are not themselves described as retained grants.
Repeated risky branches within one table/effect produce one located advisory;
audit retains their individual audiences and writers.

An initial column pass visits every sampled actor within its shared budget,
retaining each actor's first disclosure instead of stopping after the first
viewer. Later unrelated prefixes cannot replace an already observed zero-step
exposure for another sampled viewer.

Unprojected compiler-typed Row/Page payloads also witness column reads when the
returned field equals that committed physical value and its field-visibility
gate admits this viewer. This covers real media ids without manufacturing an
alternate asset ledger. Projected scalar/shape values retain the paired-world
requirement; a coincidentally matching constant is not column provenance. A
private field's redacted null is never treated as disclosure of a null value.

Absent contrary independent intent, caller-chosen role advice requires new
unconditional table authority; an additional trusted resource/ownership grant
is a scoped delegation boundary requiring its own reduction. Fixed enrollment
keeps the narrower read/delete/roster comparison. This avoids treating ordinary
self-enrollment for one's own records as privileged provisioning.

Principal-only global membership grants also participate in advice. An
unqualified viewer probe of a required unique principal denotes current
membership; authored deletion/principal reassignment can revoke it. Membership
creation receives escalation advice when it contradicts a pure caller-based
independent provisioning invariant (table insert/update/write or named operation).
Principal reassignment uses update authority including private-field ownership;
upsert creation and reassignment are judged on their separate arms. A dominating
global appointer role may reside in a separate roster, with all its qualifiers
retained when checking implication.
Ordinary account enrollment without such provisioning intent is not guessed to
be an administrator grant. Scoped/qualified membership still needs its reduction.

Paired query observations tag successful data separately from authored logical
refusals, retaining refusal codes and messages. Only engine-provided authored
rule/constraint markers qualify; incomplete reads, resource exhaustion and
ambiguous engine errors remain outside logical comparison. This prevents both
missed refusal oracles and collisions between a payload shape and a refusal.
Synthetic principal sampling separates the focal row's attribution from the
supporting roster/assignment recipient; finite enumeration still never proves
that all tuples are covered.

Within the unchanged 512-pair cap, up to 256 pairs prioritize a global-role
mutation followed by a distinct operation as the named affected principal.
Ref arguments resolve through fixture roster rows; scalar principal arguments
supply other recipients. First steps and successor operation names interleave;
remaining slots retain diagonal pairs. These are candidates only: real commits,
authorized policies and stable necessary conditions still decide witnesses.

The 16384 column budget counts call/world reservations: each world variant,
authority-judgment actor, visited observer program and generated snapshot reserves
a slot even when it declines before execution; each paired experiment reserves
four calls for primary, alternative and repeats. Typed history payload witnesses
reserve two read calls and require identical complete data on the committed image.
A failed repeat is named unsupported, never a reachable failing counterexample.

Write-arm predecessor matching uses physical unexpired rows, including rows
whose `not before` time is pending; read visibility never converts an update
into insert or hides an executed delete. Every generated and fixture column pair
compares both committed readbacks for non-protected base fields/captures and
populations, and records the actual committed alternate world. Repeatability
also checks those input worlds, not just the returned logical observations.

Whole-row paired observers use typed fixture argument products (32 per observer),
including both synthetic principals independently of the row's owner; state and
argument variants must not accidentally force every aggregate lookup to the
opposite owner. A shared 8192-call reservation budget covers primary/alternate
and repeats; exhaustion explicitly names the remaining uncovered worlds.

Column comparison also requires identical materialized observer arguments across
both worlds and repeats. Independently generated asset-input identities are an
explicit coupled-ledger blocker, not evidence about the protected column.

Time arguments sample one day before the campaign clock, the clock itself and
one day after; this controls input values, not STAGE/MIG's host clock. Singles
also retain successful unique candidates that populate an initially empty
read-protected media table and those that change an existing global role.
Up to 256 pair slots replay media creation then a role change, including changes
by another caller; actual uploaded media and both commits remain in the trace.
Remaining pair slots prefer the role-successor plan above, then diagonal pairs.
The 512-single/512-pair limits do not increase. This exercises stale media grants
without inventing asset-bearing seed rows or silently claiming longer histories.

History read observers materialize actor-owned image/file inputs before querying,
reuse those exact inputs for repeats, and clone the verified ledger into paired
absence worlds. Upload preparation failures and every failed typed/paired repeat
name their operation/invariant coverage blocker; they never silently clear an
obligation. Column pairs still decline independently materialized asset inputs
when their identities differ, as required by their fixed-input comparison.

Authority advice conjoins all supplied owned-field old-owner gates; it does not
OR ownership with the table gate. Only a statically exclusive owned-field patch
may bypass an unnamed update rule. Mixed patches and named transitions retain
the judged table gate, including private fields it needs to inspect. Insert
ownership applies to supplied fields, not omitted defaults. Named next-image
role literals participate in target-role substitution. The same calculation
governs role assignments and principal-only membership reassignment.

An update gate that implies the role/principal is unchanged is not an escalation
witness, even when its source patch explicitly supplies that field. Runtime
regressions cover owned-only, mixed, named-deny, named-no-change and named-target
restrictions for role writes, plus owned principal-only membership reassignment.

A validated empty fixture/store is also a trusted initial state. Histories do
not switch off merely because no authored rows exist: ordinary operations may
create data and subsequent authored queries may disclose it. Such traces retain
their real mutation history and authored-fixture origin. A query-only empty app
still cannot turn arbitrary seeded/synthetic rows into reachable failures. The
packed security-card proof executes its writers from empty state and requires
row and column disclosures to carry that reachable origin when the rule widens.

//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    }}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn fixture_cases(p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    let args=&p.args;
    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {
                if let Ok(value)=serde_json::from_value::<Value>(literal.clone()){out.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(value);}
            }
        }}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){collect(v,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{collect(v,out)},_=>{}}
    }
    collect(&serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap(),&mut literals);
    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
        Some(match k{
            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Int=>vec![Value::Int(0),Value::Int(1),Value::Int(2)],
            ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max}=>{let mut xs=vec![*min,1i64.clamp(*min,*max),*max];xs.sort();xs.dedup();xs.into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()},
            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
        })
    }
    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    for (name,k) in args{
        let Some(mut vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
        for literal in literals.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
            let valid=match (k,literal){
                (ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..},Value::String(s))=>s.chars().filter(|c|!c.is_whitespace()).count()>=*min as usize&&s.chars().count()<=*max as usize,
                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
            };
            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}
        }
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
        }}
        cases=next;
    }cases
}
fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str){
    let committed=|steps:&[Step]|steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&committed(&v.history)==committed(steps)){return;}
    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});
}
fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)));
    match ty{
        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
        Ty::Optional(inner)=>typed_disclosure(inner,data,table,key,column,physical),
        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure(inner,r,table,key,column,physical))),
        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure(ty,v,table,key,column,physical))),
        _=>false,
    }
}
fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    loop{let before=out.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
        if out.len()==before{return out;}
    }
}
fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
        }else{true}
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            if out.exhausted(){return;}
            let committed=steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()==committed){continue;}
            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}

            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        out.attempted+=1;
                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
                        }
                        violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
                        if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query repeatably returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
                        continue 'actors;
                    }
                }
            }
            if inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue 'actors;}
            if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history paired absence needs a referentially valid closure",inv.table));continue;}
            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
            if !reserve_observers(out,3){return;}
            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world invalid: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|observing.iter().any(|t|super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t))){
                let p=&bp.program;
                let cases=fixture_cases(p,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,4){return;}
                    let raw_args=args;
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&p.args,&raw_args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                    if args!=raw_args{
                        if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                        absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger fork: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                        if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger absence: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: ledger absence world invalid: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if readback(&absent)!=Ok(absent_rows.clone()){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired argument ledger changes the absent base world",p.name));continue;}
                    }
                    out.attempted+=2;
                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
                    if a==b{continue;}

exec

+//! Deterministic role populations validated by the ordinary seed boundary.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#132-generated-initial-worlds-and-password-only-test — generated initialization is explicit.
+use super::*;
+/// No network, credentials, development doors, durable store or scratch files.
+pub fn fixture(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
+    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fixture world not reserved; remaining initialization uncovered".into());}
+    let mut people=Vec::new();
+    for (table,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
+        let mut role_columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
+        role_columns.extend(["role","kind"].into_iter().filter(|c|t.columns.contains_key(*c)).map(str::to_owned));
+        for column in role_columns{
+            {
+                for role in super::super::privilege::role_values(bound,table,&column) {for n in 0..2 {people.push((table.clone(),column.clone(),role.clone(),format!("account:security-{table}-{role}-{n}")));}}
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for seat in histories::viewers(bound).into_iter().filter(|p|p.starts_with("service:")) {for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-{}-{n}",seat.replace(':',"-"))));}}
+    if people.is_empty(){for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-owner-{n}")));}}
+    if people.len()>64{people.truncate(64);out.unsupported.insert("generated roster capped at 64 principals; additional roles remain uncovered".into());}
+    let mut grant_tables=BTreeSet::new();
+    fn grants(rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
+        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|v|matches!(v,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{out.insert(table.clone());},
+        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{grants(r,out)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>grants(r,out),_=>{}
+    }}
+    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){grants(&t.rules.read,&mut grant_tables);}
+    let counts=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()).map(|(table,t)|{
+        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).count();(table.clone(),if own>0{own}else if grant_tables.contains(table)||t.rules.read.any_term(&|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if t.columns.get(c).is_some_and(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)))){people.len().max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3))}else{t.columns.values().filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(2).max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3)).clamp(2,8)})
+    }).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
+    let mut rows=BTreeMap::new();
+    for (table,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
+        if !counts.contains_key(table){continue;}
+        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+        if t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count()>3 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: full Cartesian combinations above three principal columns remain uncovered"));}
+        let mut population=Vec::new();
+        for n in 0..counts[table]{
+            let person=if own.is_empty(){&people[n%people.len()]}else{own[n]};
+            let mut fields=BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),Value::Id(format!("security-{table}-{n}")))]);
+            let mut principal=0;
+            for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
+                if column=="id"||t.engine_field(column)||t.recorded.contains_key(column)||t.maintained.contains_key(column){continue;}
+                let mut value=if kind.is_optional()&&n%2==0{Value::Null}else{match sample(kind.required(),4+n){Some(value)=>value,None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}.{column}: unsupported typed sample"));continue;}}};
+                if value==Value::Null&&kind.is_optional(){fields.insert(column.clone(),value);continue;}
+                match kind.required(){
+                    C::Principal=>{
+                        let linked=!own.is_empty()||grant_tables.contains(table)||t.constraints.iter().any(|constraint|matches!(constraint,Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{key,..} if key.iter().any(|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column))));
+                        value=Value::Id(if linked{if principal==0{person.3.clone()}else{people[(n+principal)%people.len()].3.clone()}}else{if (n>>principal.min(5))&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()});principal+=1;
+                    },
+                    C::Ref{table:target}=>{value=Value::Id(format!("security-{target}-{}",n%counts.get(target).copied().unwrap_or(1)));},
+                    C::Enum{..}|C::Text{..} if person.0==*table&&person.1==*column=>value=Value::String(person.2.clone()),
+                    C::Text{max}=>{let minimum=t.constraints.iter().filter_map(|c|if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=c{(field==column).then_some(*min as usize)}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(1);let suffix=format!("sample{n}");value=Value::String(format!("{suffix}{}","x".repeat(minimum.saturating_sub(suffix.len()))).chars().take(*max as usize).collect());},
+                    C::Time=>value=Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_deref()==Some(column){86_400_000}else{-1000}),
+                    _=>{},
+                }
+                fields.insert(column.clone(),value);
+            }
+            population.push(Value::Row(fields.into()));
+        }
+        rows.insert(table.clone(),population);
+    }
+    // Batch validation preserves final-exists, parent ordering and maintains.
+    if let Ok(engine)=engine_with(bound,&rows,Vec::new()){return Ok(engine);}
+    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fallback world not reserved; initialization uncovered".into());}
+    let mut engine=engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new())?;
+    let mut pending=rows.into_iter().flat_map(|(table,rows)|rows.into_iter().map(move|row|(table.clone(),row))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+    let mut reasons=BTreeMap::new();
+    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
+        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
+        for (table,mut row) in pending {
+            if !out.reserve(1){validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
+            let attempt=(||{
+                for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[&table].columns {if let Ok(value)=row.field(column){let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,value)?;if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut row{fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}}}
+                engine.seed_at([(table.clone(),row.clone())],NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;Ok::<_,String>(())
+            })();
+            if let Err(why)=attempt{reasons.insert(table.clone(),why);retry.push((table,row));}
+        }
+        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
+    }
+    for (table,why) in reasons{if pending.iter().any(|(name,_)|name==&table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: some related rows declined by ordinary validation: {why}"));}}
+    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;
+    Ok(engine)
+}

+/// Security findings that can grant authority stay ahead of repeated advice.
+pub(crate) fn summary_order(code:&str)->(Tier,u8){

 /// The first words of a note's line: what to do about it, or that nothing
 /// needs doing (wave 7 #11: "put a one-line verdict on every advisory note").
 // @ref LLP 3000.036#17.7 — the verdict leads; the explanation follows it.
@@ -111,7 +119,7 @@ pub(crate) fn render(notes: Vec<Note>) -> Vec<String> {
     let first_seen = |code: &str, unique: &[(Note, usize)]| unique.iter().position(|(n, _)| n.code == code).unwrap();

fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
}
fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
            }
        }
    }
}
fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
    for inv in &bound.invariants{
        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
        let judged;
        let inv=if inv.target=="always"{inv}else{
            let Some(stable)=stability::necessary(bound,p,inv)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: unknown/implicit policy changes need executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;};
            judged=stable;&judged
        };
        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1!="delete"&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: commit-end/implicit retirement is not an executed delete",p.name));}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1=="delete"{
            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(old,NOW)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
            }
        }
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()&&!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(r,NOW));
            if old==Some(row){continue;}
            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
            if !super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
            if inv.target=="always" {
                if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
            }else{
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row,prior)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false)){
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
                }

pub(crate) fn row_copy_ceiling(table: &Table) -> u64 {
    table.columns.iter().fold(40, |total, (name, ty)| total.saturating_add(80 + name.len() as u64).saturating_add(column_copy_ceiling(ty)))
}
pub(in crate::engine) fn column_copy_ceiling(ty: &ColumnType) -> u64 {
    match ty {
        ColumnType::Id | ColumnType::Principal | ColumnType::Ref { .. } => 144,
        ColumnType::Text { max } => u64::from(*max) * 4,
        ColumnType::Bytes { max } => u64::from(*max),
        ColumnType::Enum { variants } => variants.iter().map(|v| v.len() as u64).max().unwrap_or(0),
        ColumnType::Optional(inner) => column_copy_ceiling(inner),
        ColumnType::List { of, max } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, max, .. } => u64::from(*max).saturating_mul(std::mem::size_of::<Value>() as u64 + column_copy_ceiling(of)),
        // Canonical JSON bytes bound owned strings/bytes and array slots at every
        // depth. Object subtrees, like image/video facts, are shared Arc values.
        ColumnType::Json { max_bytes } => u64::from(*max_bytes) * (std::mem::size_of::<Value>() as u64 + 1),
        ColumnType::Image { .. } | ColumnType::Video { .. } | ColumnType::File { .. } | ColumnType::Int | ColumnType::IntRange { .. } | ColumnType::Decimal { .. } | ColumnType::Money { .. } | ColumnType::Bool | ColumnType::Time => 0,
    }
}

impl Runner<'_> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — pay for both hidden contributions before the old image; no predicate-dependent success/budget channel.
    pub(in crate::engine) fn reserve_maintains(&mut self, table_name: &str, table: &Table) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let maintains: Vec<_> = self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.source == table_name && m.private.is_none() && m.row_key.is_none()).collect();
        let deferred = self.device && maintains.iter().any(|m| self.schema.receipt_dependencies.contains_key(&m.target) || self.schema.tables[&m.target].sync.is_none());
        self.reservation_deferred |= self.device && deferred;
        if !deferred && (table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable() || self.context.is_system()
            || !maintains.iter().any(|m| m.filter.is_some() || !m.computed.is_empty() || table.is_expiring())) { return Ok(()); }
        let price = self.source_price(table_name, crate::schema::reservations::AGGREGATES)?;
        self.charge_price(price)?;
        self.maintain_heap = price[4];
        self.maintain_prepaid = true;
        Ok(())
    }
    pub(crate) fn charge_maintain(&mut self, kind: MeterKind, amount: u64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if !self.maintain_prepaid { self.meter.charge(kind, amount)?; }
        Ok(())
    }
}

+fn role_candidates(bound:&Bound,target:&str,row:&snapback4_core::schema::Table)->Vec<RuleExpr>{
+    use snapback4_core::{schema::ColumnType as C,Value};
+    let mut out=Vec::new();
+    for (roster,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
+        let mut columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,roster);
+        columns.extend(["role","kind"].into_iter().filter(|c|t.columns.contains_key(*c)).map(str::to_owned));
+        for column in columns {for role in super::privilege::role_values(bound,roster,&column) {
+            for (index,idx) in t.indexes.iter().filter(|(_,idx)|idx.unique){
+                let mut key=Vec::new();let mut principals=0;
+                for component in &idx.components {
+                    let Some(c)=component.column()else{break;};
+                    let term=if c==&column{RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(role.clone()))}else{match t.columns.get(c).map(C::required){
+                        Some(C::Principal)=>{principals+=1;RuleTerm::Viewer},
+                        Some(C::Ref{table}) if table==target=>RuleTerm::Field("id".into()),
+                        Some(C::Ref{table})=>{
+                            let refs=row.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table:parent} if parent==table)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+                            if refs.len()!=1{break;}RuleTerm::Field(refs[0].0.clone())
+                        },_=>break,
+                    }};key.push(term);
+                }
+                if principals!=1||key.len()!=idx.components.len(){continue;}
+                let equalities=if idx.components.iter().any(|c|c.column()==Some(&column)){Vec::new()}else{vec![(column.clone(),RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(role.clone())))]};
+                let policy=RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster.clone(),index:index.clone(),key,provenance:snapback4_core::ir::KeyProvenance::Computed,equalities};
+                if !out.contains(&policy){out.push(policy);}break;
+            }
+        }}
+    }out
+}
